## 🤔 Seedance 2.0 nedir?

**Seedance 2.0**, **ByteDance** tarafından geliştirilen bir video oluşturma modelidir. Endüstrinin **eş zamanlı dört-modlu giriş** destekleyen ilk modeli — görüntü, video, ses ve metin.

**Key Features:**
- 🎥 **Metinden Videoya** — Metin açıklamalarından video oluşturma
- 🖼️ **Görüntüden Videoya** — Statik görüntüleri dinamik videolara dönüştürme
- 📹 **Videodan Videoya** — Mevcut videoları dönüştürme ve genişletme
- 🎵 **Ses Güdümlü** — Ses girişiyle yönlendirilen video oluşturma
- 📐 **1080p çözünürlüğe kadar**, 4-15 saniye süre
- 🔊 **Otomatik seslendirme ve puanlama** — Otomatik anlatım ve arka plan müziği

### No. 1: Seedance 2.0: 15 Saniyelik Sinematik Japon Romantik Kısa Filmi

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Seedance 2.0 için sinematik, ultra gerçekçi bir Japon lise saf aşk kısa filmi oluşturmak üzere tasarlanmış, son derece ayrıntılı, 15 saniyelik çok sahneli bir komut istemi. Komut istemi, sahne ayarını (boş sınıf, sıcak altın rengi güneş ışığı, toz zerrecikleri), kamera hareketlerini, karakter tutarlılığını (deformasyon/kayma yok), ince mikro ifadeleri, senkronize nefes alma/dudak hareketlerini, diyalogları ve ses efektlerini (ağustos böcekleri, kalem sesi, düşük frekanslı kalp atışı, yumuşak piyano) belirtir. Anlatı, yazan bir kız ile onu gizlice izleyen bir erkek arasındaki yoğun, garip ve samimi duygusal gerilime odaklanarak utangaç bir yüzleşmeyle sonuçlanır.

#### 📝 İstem

```
15 saniyelik sinematik Japon draması, saf aşk temalı, muğlak kısa film, ultra gerçekçi kalite, öğleden sonra boş bir sınıfta sıcak altın güneş ışığı, panjurlardan yan yana duran sıralara süzülüyor, ışık huzmelerinde ince toz zerrecikleri yavaşça süzülüyor, eski ahşap sıralar, son derece doğal, ince hareketler, nefes alma ve göz gerilimi, karakterler deformasyon, kayma veya bozulma olmadan baştan sona tutarlı yüzler, giysiler ve saç stilleri koruyor, nefes almayla senkronize gerçek, hafif göğüs kalkıp inmesi, sığ alan derinliği, kremsi bulanık arka plan, sıcak film greni, 8K keskinlik, Japon gençliğinin bastırılmış kalp çarpıntısı yaratan boğucu atmosferi.
0-4 saniye: Masaüstünün orta çekiminden, yan yana oturan iki kişinin profilinin yakın çekimine doğru son derece yavaş bir yakınlaştırma. Yazlık okul üniforması giymiş saf bir kız, başı eğik bir şekilde not yazmaya odaklanmış, uzun siyah saçları ve kulaklarının yanındaki dağınık saç telleri hafif bir esintiyle nazikçe havalanıyor, uzun kirpikleri ince gölgeler düşürüyor, cildi doğal olarak pembe ve narin, konsantrasyonla ağzının kenarında hafif, istemsiz bir yukarı kıvrılma, hafif ve düzenli nefes alma.
4-9 saniye: Erkeğin yakın çekimine geçiş. Okul üniformasının yakası hafifçe gevşek, dirseğini masaya dayamış ve gizlice başını çevirip kıza bakıyor, gözleri nazik, bastırılmış bir sevgi ve şefkatle dolu, göz bebekleri hafifçe büyümüş, Adem elması nazikçe hareket ediyor. Aniden kızın kaleminin durduğunu fark edince, hızla ve telaşla başını çevirip kendi notlarına bakıyormuş gibi yapıyor, kulak memeleri hızla hafifçe kızarıyor, kalemi tutan parmak uçları hafifçe titriyor, ara sıra perçemlerinin altından kıza bakıyor, nefesi hafifçe düzensizleşiyor ve sakin kalmak için dudaklarını sıkıca bastırıyor.
9-15 saniye: Aynı karede her iki yüzün aşırı yakın çekimi, yavaş çekimde gözler aniden buluşuyor: kız yavaşça başını çeviriyor, önce şaşkın bir sürpriz gösteriyor, sonra hızla ve utangaçça 0.3 saniye boyunca başını eğiyor, alt dudağını nazikçe ısırıyor, yanakları ve kulak memeleri anında kiraz çiçeği pembesiyle doluyor, nemli kirpikleri utangaçça yukarı bakıp tekrar onun bakışlarıyla buluşuyor, aynı zamanda yumuşak ve utangaçça fısıldıyor, "...Neye bakıyorsun?"; erkek tamamen donup kalıyor, göz bebekleri büyüyor ve 0.4 saniye boyunca şaşkın kalıyor, sonra telaşla ve sessizce kekeleyerek yanıt veriyor, "H-hiçbir şey...". Kız daha da sessizce fısıldıyor, dudağını ısırıp tekrar ona bakıyor, fısıldamaya devam ediyor, "...Yalancı.". Erkek duraklıyor, sonra nazikçe iç çekiyor ve fısıldıyor, "...Sadece sana bakıyordum.", ağzının kenarı yavaşça utangaç, nazik, çarpık bir gülümsemeye dönüşüyor, gözlerinin kenarlarında ince çizgiler beliriyor ve nefesi belirgin bir şekilde derinleşiyor. Görünmez bir akım, yüzleri arasındaki muğlak gerilimi çekiyor gibi, birbirlerinin nefes sıcaklığını paylaşıyorlar, arka plan tamamen kremsi, rüya gibi ışık noktaları, sıcak haleler ve ince hava parçacıkları katmanlarına dönüşüyor.
Dudak senkronizasyonu doğal ve hassas, duygusal mikro titremeler ve nefes alma senkronize, diyalog utangaç bir tonla düşük enerjili fısıltılar, 200-400 milisaniye arasında doğal kısa duraklamalar, ağız konuşurken sadece hafifçe hareket ediyor, abartı veya robotik bir his yok, mükemmel doğal dudak senkronizasyonu ve duygusal özgünlük.
Genel Ses Efektleri: Uzak yaz cırcır böceklerinin hafif cıvıltısı, kalemin kağıda değme sesi, kalp atışlarının neredeyse duyulmayan düşük frekanslı nabzı, sonunda çok hafif, havadar bir piyano sesine karışıyor. Diyalog, fısıltılar olarak sahneye tamamen doğal bir şekilde entegre edilmiş, kızın sesi yumuşak ve utangaç, erkeğin sesi telaşlı kekelemeden nazik bir tona geçiyor.
Karakter kimliği baştan sona korunuyor, gerçek ince baş eğmeleri, göz hareketleri ve nefes senkronizasyonu, metin, filigran veya altyazı yok, saf Japon tarzı gençlik gizli aşk kalp çarpıntısı gerilimi.
```

### No. 2: Hollywood Haute Couture Fantezi Video İstemleri

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Hollywood Haute Couture Fantezi filmi oluşturmak üzere tasarlanmış, Seedance 2.0 için ayrıntılı, çok sahneli bir video oluşturma istemi. İstem; stil, çözünürlük (8K), oluşturma motoru (Unreal Engine 5), süre (15 saniye) ve sıvı mavi-beyaz porselen içinde bir modelin mürekkep yıkamalı kırlangıçlara dönüşerek parçalanmasını içeren, 3D bir sıvı mürekkep girdabıyla sonuçlanan üç farklı kamera/aksiyon sekansı belirtir.

#### 📝 İstem

```
[Tarz] Hollywood Haute Couture Fantastik gişe rekorları kıran film, 8K ultra net, Fotogerçekçi, Yüksek moda Editoryal Tarz, Unreal Engine 5 akışkan işleme, görsel illüzyon. [Süre] 15 saniye. [Sahne] Sonsuz, gerçek hayat Salar de Uyuni (Gökyüzü Aynası) tuz düzlüğü. Gökyüzü baskıcı koyu bulutlarla dolu ve zemin her şeyi bir ayna gibi mükemmel bir şekilde yansıtıyor, genel resim minimalist, soğuk bir ton sunuyor. [00:00-00:05] Çekim 1: Haute Couture Giriş ve Porselen Cilt. Kamera konumu: Aşırı düşük açılı yukarı doğru çekim, ultra telefoto lens yakınlaştırma. Aksiyon: Son derece tanınabilir, yüksek moda bir yüze sahip Asyalı bir kadın model, su yüzeyinde havalı bir şekilde yürüyor. Efekt: Kumaş değil, akan, gerçek Sıvı Mavi-Beyaz Porselen'den yapılmış uzun bir elbise giyiyor. Yürüdükçe, etek gerçek seramik gibi çıtırdayan bir çarpışma sesi çıkarıyor, yüzeyinde akıcı bir parlaklık var. Geleneksel mavi-beyaz desenler, beyaz porselen dokulu etek üzerinde canlıymış gibi hareket ediyor. [00:05-00:10] Çekim 2: Fiziksel Parçalanma ve Mürekkep Yıkama İnişi. Kamera konumu: Yüzün aşırı yakın çekimi, odak hızla geri çekiliyor. Aksiyon: Model aniden durur, kameraya soğukça bakar ve parmaklarını çıtırdatır. Efekt: Parmaklar şıklatıldığı anda, mavi-beyaz porselen elbisesi düşmez, ancak anında binlerce son derece fotogerçekçi Mürekkep Yıkama Kırlangıcına dönüşür. Bu kırlangıçlar gerçek su damlacıkları ve mürekkep izleri taşır, havada siyah akışkan artçıllar sürükler, etrafında çılgınca dönerler. [00:10-00:15] Çekim 3: Boyutsal Çözülme ve Uçurum Yansıması. Kamera konumu: Yüksek irtifa tepeden çekim, kamera hızla döner ve alçalır. Aksiyon: Mürekkep yıkama kırlangıçları sürüsü, modelin ayaklarının altındaki aynalı göl suyuna dalar. Efekt: Başlangıçta katı olan tuz gölünün yüzey gerilimi anında kaybolur. Tüm son derece gerçekçi dünya, berrak suya damlatılan konsantre mürekkep gibi şiddetli bir şekilde kanamaya ve çözülmeye başlar. Gerçek koyu bulutlar ve modelin figürü, kamerayı siyah ve beyaz iç içe geçmiş bir uçuruma tamamen yutan son derece görkemli bir 3D Akışkan Mürekkep Girdabına dönüşür.
```

### No. 3: Modern Kırsal Estetik Şifa Veren Kısa Film Video İstemleri

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Seedance 2.0 için, Modern Kırsal Estetik tarzında şifa veren, sinematik bir kısa film oluşturmak üzere detaylı, üç sahneli bir komut istemi. Tarzı (Sinematik Reklam, 4K/8K, Aşırı Makro, doğal ışık, ASMR), ortamı (bahçe manzaralı modern bir açık mutfak), karakteri (keten giysili, odaklanmış bir yaratıcı) ve üç sahne için belirli eylemleri belirtir: domates toplama, hassas kesim ve sessiz bir yeme anı.

#### 📝 İstem

```
[Stil]
Modern Kırsal Estetik, Sinematik Ticari kalite, Sony A7S3/sinema kamerasıyla çekildi, 4K/8K ultra net, Aşırı Makro, doğal şeffaf aydınlatma, iyileştirici ASMR, tarihi kostüm draması hissi yok.

[Sahne]
Bakımlı, modern bir çiftlik evinin açık mutfağı, arka planda yemyeşil bir sebze bahçesi, parlak güneş ışığı.

[Karakter]
Modern Kırsal Yaratıcı, siyah uzun saçları ahşap bir saç tokasıyla gelişigüzel toplanmış, koyu mavi rahat bir keten kıyafet giymiş, sade makyajlı, odaklanmış ve huzurlu gözler.

[Çekim Detayları]
[00:00-00:05] Çekim 1: Sabah Hasadı (Tazelik)
Görseller: Yüksek çözünürlüklü yakın çekim. Sabah güneşi bitkilere yandan arkadan aydınlatma ile vuruyor.
Eylem: Yaratıcının çıplak elleri (uzun, temiz parmaklar) asmadan parlak kırmızı, üzerinde parlayan çiy damlaları olan bir domatesi koparıyor.
Detaylar: Aşırı keskin odak, domates yüzeyindeki tüyleri ve kayan su damlacıklarının yörüngesini net bir şekilde gösteriyor. Arka plan bulanık, yüksek kaliteli yeşil.

[00:05-00:10] Çekim 2: Aşırı Zanaatkarlık (Zanaat)
Görseller: İç mekan ocak alanı, hayat dolu ama tertemiz.
Eylem: Yaratıcı sebzeleri kesiyor, hareketleri ustaca ve hassas (performans amaçlı değil).
Detaylar: Makro lens, bıçak ağzının malzemeleri kestiği anı, suyun sıçramasını yakalıyor. Ardından toprak ocakta titreşen turuncu aleve geçiyor, ışık ve gölge sıcak ve gerçek.

[000:10-00:15] Çekim 3: Sakin Zaman (An)
Görseller: Tam çekim/Orta çekim.
Eylem: Avludaki ahşap uzun masaya narin bir ev yemeği konulur. Yaratıcı sessizce oturur, başıboş bir saçı nazikçe düzeltir ve bir lokma yemek alır.
Atmosfer: Arkadan aydınlatmaya karşı buhar yavaşça yükselir, sahne o kadar sessizdir ki rüzgarın sesini neredeyse duyabilirsiniz, modern insanların özlemini duyduğu nihai rahatlama hissini sergiler.
```

### No. 4: Demon Slayer Live-Action Savaş İstemleri Seedance 2.0 için

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Seedance 2.0 için, Demon Slayer tarzı bir savaşın (Su Nefesi vs. Şimşek Nefesi) 15 saniyelik canlı aksiyon uyarlamasını oluşturan, ayrıntılı, yüksek enerjili bir video istemi. İstem, stili (Hollywood canlı aksiyon manga uyarlaması, karanlık samuray, 4K, aşırı hızlı kesmeler, parçacık ışık efektleri), ortamı (gece sisli orman) ve karakterlerin eylemlerini, güçlenme sekanslarını ve son çarpışmayı detaylandıran üç farklı çekimi belirtiyor.

#### 📝 İstem

```
Canlı Aksiyon Manga Uyarlaması · Nefes Tekniği Kararlı Savaş (15 saniye · Ultra Yakıcı Özel Efektler Versiyonu)
【Temel Odak】: Su Nefesi (Mavi Su Ejderhası) VS Şimşek Nefesi (Altın Şimşek), canlı aksiyon aşırı hızlı düello.
【Tarz】: Hollywood canlı aksiyon manga uyarlaması film kalitesi, karanlık samuray tarzı, 4K ultra net, aşırı hızlı kesimler, parçacık ışık efektleri patlaması, kan yok.
【Süre】: 15 saniye
【Sahne】: Ay ışığı altında sisli orman, çamurlu zemin, düşen yapraklar.
[00:00-00:05] Çekim 1: Su Melodisi Prelüdü · Başlangıç Formu (Yüklenme Hissi)
Görseller: Yeşil ve siyah kareli haori (ceket) giyen genç bir samuray, ay ışığı altında ağırlık merkezini alçaltır, kılıcını iki eliyle tutar.
Aksiyon: Derin bir nefes alır ve çevresindeki hava anında katılaşır. Kılıcını çekerken, yüksek basınçlı su akışından yoğunlaşmış dev bir mavi su ejderhası, havadan belirir, vücudunun ve kılıcının etrafında hızla döner, akan suyun kükremesini yayar.
Özel Efekt Detayları: Su akışı gerçekçi bir sıçrama hissine sahiptir, karanlık ormanı aydınlatır.
[00:05-00:10] Çekim 2: Şimşek Flaş · İlerleme (Aşırı Hız Hissi)
Görseller: Karşısındaki rakibi, sarı üçgen desenli haori giyen sarışın bir kılıç ustası, vücudunu aşırı derecede alçaltır, bir Iaijutsu (kılıç çekme tekniği) duruşu alır.
Aksiyon: Zemin aniden patlar. Tüm vücudu anında gözle görülmeyen bir hızla ağaçların arasından 'Z' şeklinde hızla kırılan ve ilerleyen göz kamaştırıcı altın bir şimşek artığına dönüşür.
Özel Efekt Detayları: Geçtiği yerlerde altın elektrik arkları ve yanmış düşen yapraklar kalır.
[00:10-00:15] Çekim 3: Su-Şimşek Çarpışması · Son Ses (Nihai Hareketlerin Çatışması)
Görseller: Aşırı hızlı kafa kafaya çarpışma. Genç samuray, saldırıyı karşılamak için dev mavi su ejderhasını aşağı doğru savurur ve şimşeğe dönüşen sarışın kılıç ustası ona çarpar.
Aksiyon: İki kılıç çerçevenin merkezinde şiddetle çarpışır.
Özel Efekt Gösterisi: Mavi su ejderhası ve altın şimşek anında patlar, dışarıya doğru yayılan devasa bir su-şimşek enerji fırtınası oluşturur. Çevredeki büyük ağaçlar enerji dalgasıyla ikiye ayrılır ve çamur, su ve ışık kamerayı gizler. Sahne aşırı derecede göz kamaştırıcı mavi, sarı ve beyaz bir ışıkla sona erer.
```

### No. 5: Seedance 2.0: 80 Yaşındaki Rapçinin MV'si

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Seedance 2.0 için 80 yaşında bir kadının yer aldığı 16:9 yatay sokak rap müzik videosu (MV) oluşturmak üzere hazırlanmış ayrıntılı, 15 saniyelik bir komut istemi. Komut istemi; stil (neon mor/mavi soğuk tonlar, patlayıcı atmosfer), karakter görünümü (gümüş saç, deri ceket, hip-hop aksesuarları), sahne dökümü (0-3 saniye giriş, 3-7 saniye rap yapma, 7-11 saniye dans etme, 11-15 saniye doruk/sonuç), belirli rap şarkı sözleri, kamera teknikleri (düşük açı, 360 derece dönüş, hızlı kesmeler) ve ses tasarımını (Trap elektronik müzik, güçlü 808 davulları) belirtir.

#### 📝 İstem

```
16:9 yatay ekran, sokak rap MV tarzı, neon mor ve mavi soğuk tonlar, patlayıcı havalı ve sert atmosfer. 0-3 saniye: Orta çekim yakınlaşma, yanıp sönen neon ışıklarıyla şehir sokağı gece sahnesi, grafiti duvarının önünde 80 yaşında gümüş saçlı bir kadın duruyor, kısa gümüş-beyaz saçları düzgünce geriye taranmış, belirgin kare yüz hatları, şakaklara doğru eğimli kılıç gibi kaşlar, elektrik gibi keskin gözler, gözlerinin köşelerindeki kırışıklıklar zamanın nişaneleri gibi, ağzının köşesinde kendinden emin bir gülümseme, beyaz baskılı bir tişörtün (göğsünde büyük siyah harflerle "YOLO") üzerine siyah deri ceket + siyah kargo pantolon + beyaz yüksek bilekli spor ayakkabılar giymiş, boynunda kalın altın zincir kolye, bileğinde gümüş bilezik, iki eliyle bir mikrofon tutuyor, BGM'nin güçlü davul vuruşları başlıyor, yaşlı kadının gözleri keskinleşiyor ve dudakları Rap yapmaya başlamak için açılıyor. 3-7 saniye: Orta çekim + yakın çekim geçişi, yaşlı kadın rap yapmaya başlıyor, son derece güçlü bir ritim duygusuyla, gümüş saçları başını sallama hareketleriyle uçuşuyor, bir eliyle mikrofonu tutarken diğer eliyle ritme uygun hareketler yapıyor - işaret parmağı kamerayı gösteriyor, avuç içi ritmi yukarı ve aşağı kesiyor, hip-hop hareketleri yapıyor, hareketler akıcı ve pürüzsüz, gözleri keskin ve doğrudan kameraya bakıyor, kırışıklıkları ifadesiyle canlı bir şekilde zıplıyor, dudakları hızla açılıp kapanarak şarkı sözlerini tükürüyor: [Rap Sözleri] "Seksen yaşındaki bacaklar, sizden daha iyi zıplayabilir! Gümüş saçlar uçuşuyor, bu benim gururum! Bana yaşlı demeyin, Flow'um sizinkinden daha iyi, siz rap yaparken ben disko dinliyordum!" (Hızlı tempo, güçlü ritim, sert tavır) Hızlı kesimler: yüz yakın çekimleri, el hareketleri, tüm vücudun sallanması, yan silüetler, BGM ritmiyle senkronize. 7-11 saniye: Dans bölümü, kamera tüm vücudu göstermek için geri çekiliyor, yaşlı kadın dans etmeye başlıyor - önce klasik hip-hop zıplaması, ardından düzgün bir sokak dansı freeze'i, ardından omuzlardan ayak parmaklarına yayılan bir vücut dalgası ve ardından hızlı bir ayak çalışması, hareketler temiz ve keskin, neon ışıkların altında gümüş saçlar uçuşuyor, deri ceket havada dalgalanıyor, dans ederken Rap yapmaya devam ediyor: [Rap Sözleri] "Bacaklar ve ayaklar çevik, hız yavaş değil, sözlerim zamana kazınmış! Siz telefonlarla oynarsınız, ben ritimlerle oynarım, seksen yıllık hayat, bu dizeye yazılmış!" (Daha hızlı ritim, daha güçlü ton) Aşağıdan yukarıya çekim + 360 derece çevreleyen çekim, yaşlı kadının havalı ve sert dans hareketlerini yakalıyor. 11-15 saniye: Zirve sonu, yaşlı kadın havalı bir dönüş yapıyor, gümüş saçları havada yay çiziyor, kameraya dönüyor ve parmağıyla "şşş" işareti yapıyor, ardından dudakları mikrofona yaklaşıyor, son dizeyi alçak, manyetik bir sesle söylüyor: [Gerçek Sözler] "Zaman asla bir güzelliği yenemez, ben sadece gençliği deneyimleme şeklimi değiştirdim..." (Yavaş ritim, derin duygu, kalıcı bitiş) Kamera yavaşça yakınlaşarak yaşlı kadının gözlerinin yakın çekimini yapıyor, gözlerinin köşelerindeki kırışıklıklar hepsi birer hikaye, bakışları hala keskin ama aynı zamanda bir nezaket ipucuyla, BGM zirvede aniden duruyor, kare yaşlı kadının havalı ama hafifçe nazik gülümsemesinde donuyor, vinyet + neon mor ışık halesi.
```

### No. 6: Sinematik Sokak Yarışı Sekansı İstemi

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)

#### 📖 Açıklama

Seedance 2.0 kullanarak gece vakti sinematik bir sokak yarışı sekansı oluşturmak için kamera hareketlerini, çekim zamanlamasını (0-12 sn), çevre detaylarını ve istenen görsel stili (Hızlı ve Öfkeli esintili) belirten detaylı bir istem.

#### 📝 İstem

```
gece vakti sinematik sokak yarışı sekansı, yüksek performanslı bir arabanın içindeki odaklanmış sürücü direksiyonu kavrıyor, yoğun göz odaklanması, ön cama yansıyan şehir ışıkları, ani hızlanma öncesi artan gerilim

kamera: kesintisiz geçişlere sahip hızlı çok açılı sistem, iç mekan yakın çekim → omuz üstü → dış takip → düşük zemin çekimleri, ultra dinamik kamera hareketi, hızlı panlar + hız rampası geçişleri + hareket bulanıklığı maskeleme kesmeleri, sürekli akış illüzyonu

(0-2 sn) sürücünün iç mekan yakın çekimi, el vites kolunu sıkıyor, hafif nefes alışverişi, parlayan gösterge paneli ışıkları
(2-4 sn) omuz üstü çekim, neon ışıklı şehre uzanan yol, artan motor titreşimi
(4-6 sn) NOS düğmesine basan parmağın aşırı yakın çekimi, anlık ateşleme tepkisi
(6-8 sn) patlayıcı hızlanma, kamera dış yan takip çekimine geçiyor, araba şiddetli bir hız artışıyla ileri fırlıyor
(8-10 sn) asfalta yakın ultra düşük zemin çekimi, aşırı hızla dönen tekerlekler, yanından hızla geçen çevre
(10-12 sn) dar sokaklarda yüksek hızlı kovalamaca, keskin dönüşler, açılar arasında hızlı kamera panları, hızı artıran yansımalar ve ışık izleri

Yoğun kentsel gece ortamı, neon ışıkları yansıtan ıslak asfalt, tünel geçişleri, hızla geçen sokak lambaları, yüksek tempolu şehir atmosferi
Ultra gerçekçi, Hızlı ve Öfkeli esintili enerji, fotogerçekçi aydınlatma, yoğun hareket bulanıklığı, yüksek kontrastlı neon yansımaları, sinematik alan derinliği, aşırı hız hissi, akıcı geçişler, bozulma yok, esneme yok
```

Kael looks amazed.

Kael:

Silence.

Lyra:
 "Then why did the forest bring him?"

Kael shows the glowing seed.

Kael:

Lyra reaches toward the seed.

The magic reacts.

Lyra teaches Kael about the forest.
Lyra:

Drawing each other

Watching stars beside a magical lake

Flying above clouds

Lyra:
 "Your world is beautiful."

Kael smiles.

Kael:
 "Only because I finally have

```
Create a 15 second cinematic desert survival sequence in 16:9 with grounded live action realism, natural body mechanics, accurate dust, convincing vehicle suspension, subtle film grain, restrained color grading, and seamless shot continuity. The setting is a sun bleached basin surrounded by dry brown mountains beneath a clear cyan sky. Sparse shrubs, cracked asphalt, faded yellow road lines, heat shimmer, and windblown grit make the landscape feel harsh and real. 0 to 3 seconds: Begin with a low centered wide shot from the middle of an empty highway using a 40 millimeter lens. A weathered blue camper van approaches from far distance, growing steadily larger without unnatural acceleration. Keep the camera nearly locked with faint wind vibration. Hard afternoon sunlight creates crisp shadows and warm windshield reflections. Cut inside the van for a brief front mounted shot. A tired middle aged woman drives while a bearded man watches the road, with two younger passengers and a scruffy cream dog visible among blankets, hanging utensils, maps, and handmade decorations. Use gentle road vibration, imperfect focus breathing, sunlight leaking through dusty glass, and believable nervous glances. 3 to 8 seconds: Cut to an extremely low roadside angle beside dormant alien creatures scattered across the asphalt. They resemble enormous desert arthropods built from cracked shell, leathery tissue, tangled rootlike fibers, dried mud, and embedded stones. The van passes behind them with realistic wheel motion and a pressure wave that stirs dust and loose fibers. Hold after the van exits. A small claw twitches in the foreground, then one massive creature slowly lifts its armored head. Its weight compresses its jointed legs, gravel shifts beneath it, dust falls from its body, and its wet mouth opens with restrained animal behavior. Make a subtle fearful camera push as the creature rises into a dominant low angle silhouette. 8 to 12 seconds: Hard cut to a distant elevated view across a grassy desert plain. A rugged male traveler descends beneath a huge black organic parachute shaped like four heavy flower petals. The canopy is stretched translucent membrane with irregular holes, scarred veins, and long elastic tendrils connected to his harness. Wind deforms it asymmetrically. His arms fight for balance, one foot is bare, the other wears a worn boot, and his clothing flaps with accurate drag. Use a long lens that compresses the mountains and makes the descent feel strangely graceful. 12 to 15 seconds: Shift into a fast lateral tracking shot near ground level as the traveler skims above shrubs, briefly disappearing behind a bush before reappearing. His legs bicycle in panic, his body swings naturally under tension, and his shadow races over uneven ground. He touches down awkwardly, stumbles two steps, then braces as the enormous black canopy collapses behind and around him

### Realistic Korean Man Park Vlog

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A highly detailed video generation prompt designed to create a realistic UGC vlog featuring a young Korean man in a public park, including storyboard instructions and specific camera effects like handheld shaking.

#### 📝 İstem

```
CAMERA:
DV 16mm tape camcorder handheld footage feel. Point of view of JAY holding the camera directly in hand. Maintain hand shake, misaligned framing, delayed focus pull, clumsy zoom in/out, occasional self-cam framing where the face gets cut off, and imperfect shots that lose the subject. Every shot is JAY holding the camcorder himself, filming in selfie-cam or first-person style. The camcorder itself never appears on screen.

LOOK:
DV 16mm tape camcorder footage look. Soft, slightly blurry digital tape quality, faint tape noise, highlights that bloom subtly in bright daylight, gentle auto-exposure shifts as the camera moves between sunlight and shade, muted color contrast, realistic skin tones.

STYLE:
Relaxed daytime vlog with a calm, genuine, low-energy-but-happy vibe. Slightly tired after a long walk or outdoor workout, but satisfied and enjoying the fresh air. Handheld throughout, natural pauses between lines, occasional quiet breathing, authentic unposed expressions, documentary realism.

Character

JAY — a handsome Korean man in his 20s. Thick black hair slightly tousled from being outdoors, sharp yet warm Korean facial features, healthy glowing skin with a light sheen of sweat, expressive eyes, athletic slim build. Wearing a modest long-sleeve athletic top or lightweight hoodie, loose-fit joggers or athletic pants, comfortable sneakers. Fully covered arms and torso. No jewelry.

Setting

A peaceful public park during the daytime under natural sunlight. Wide green lawns, mature trees casting soft shadows, paved walking paths, park benches, trimmed bushes, birds occasionally flying overhead, gentle breeze moving the leaves, distant people walking or jogging naturally in the background without drawing attention, bright blue sky with soft clouds. Everything feels calm, open, and naturally lit.

Storyboard

(~2s, camera propped against a park bench, medium shot)
He walks into frame breathing slightly heavier from a walk or light workout, wipes his forehead with the back of his hand, gives a small relaxed smile.

JAY:
""Okay... just finished my walk, such a nice day.""

(~2s, handheld, slow pan across the park)
Camera slowly drifts across the green trees, walking paths, and open lawn before returning to him.

JAY (off-screen, softly):
""It's so peaceful here today.""

(~2s, medium handheld, beside a park bench)
He grabs his water bottle from the bench, takes a long drink, then exhales comfortably afterward.

JAY:
""Needed that so bad.""

(~1.5s, macro insert, shallow DOF)
Close-up on his hand wiping condensation from the water bottle, sunlight reflecting off tiny water droplets. No dialogue—only soft breathing and subtle ambient park sounds.

(~2s, medium handheld, open grassy area)
He props the camera against the bench, steps back into frame, casually stretches, then playfully does a few quick dance moves or light footwork before laughing
```

```
Main Subject: Young Korean woman, 23, natural everyday appearance, long black hair worn loose and slightly messy from the sea breeze, realistic skin texture with lightly sun-kissed cheeks, minimal makeup, cheerful and carefree personality. Wearing a loose white oversized linen shirt over a light blue tank top, beige linen shorts, white sandals, a woven straw hat, and a small canvas tote bag. Maintain consistent identity, clothing, hairstyle, and appearance throughout.

Location: A quiet beach on Jeju Island during an early summer morning. Soft golden sunlight, gentle ocean waves, black volcanic rocks, clear turquoise water, seashells scattered along the sand, a few distant fishermen, and a peaceful, uncrowded shoreline.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior, natural body language, authentic vacation atmosphere, warm morning light, rich environmental details.

00:00–00:03
She walks barefoot along the shoreline carrying her sandals in one hand. Small waves wash over her feet as the camera briefly focuses on the water before finding her face.

00:03–00:06
She crouches to pick up a unique seashell, smiling as she brushes the sand away with her thumb. The operator moves closer, causing slight autofocus breathing.

00:06–00:09
Standing on a black volcanic rock, she watches the horizon while the sea breeze moves her hair and oversized shirt naturally.

She skips a flat stone across the calm water, laughing softly after it bounces several times. The camera shakes as the operator reacts a little too late.

00:12–00:15
She turns toward the camcorder with a warm smile, places the seashell inside her tote bag, and continues walking down the empty beach. The recording drifts toward the waves and abruptly cuts to black.

Audio: Natural ambience only–gentle waves, seabirds, light wind, distant fishermen talking, footsteps on wet sand, water splashing, and rustling fabric. No music. No narration.

Goal: A forgotten MiniDV home video from summer 2004, capturing a peaceful morning walk on Jeju Island that feels warm, imperfect, nostalgic, and completely authentic.

### Pixar Style Pigeon Chase Animation

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A long-form episodic video prompt for a Pixar-inspired 3D animated short film about a man and his pigeon nemesis.

#### 📝 İstem

```
MY NEMESIS IS A PIGEON” Style: Pixar-Inspired 3D Animation • Comedy • Family-Friendly • Viral Short Film ⸻ 🚪 ACT 1 — THE DAILY FEAR Dave is getting ready to leave his apartment. But something feels wrong. Before stepping outside, he carefully checks every direction. Left. Right. The sky. Behind a nearby tree. Even under a parked car. He’s clearly done this many times before. The audience immediately wonders: “What is this guy afraid of?” Dave finally relaxes. Maybe today will be different. ⸻ 🐦 ACT 2 — THE ENEMY RETURNS The moment Dave steps outside, everything changes. A shadow slowly moves across his face. He looks up. Perched high above him sits an angry pigeon. Not an ordinary pigeon. This pigeon looks like a movie villain. It stares directly at Dave. Waiting. Watching. The audience instantly understands: These two have history. ⸻ 🏃 ACT 3 — THE CHASE Without warning, the pigeon launches. Dave immediately panics. The chase begins. He runs through the city. Dodging pedestrians. Jumping around benches. Desperately trying to escape. The pigeon follows every move perfectly. No matter what Dave does, the pigeon always seems one step ahead. The audience starts laughing because the pigeon behaves less like a bird and more like a supervillain. ⸻ 🚌 ACT 4 — FALSE HOPE Dave spots a bus and jumps onboard. Finally safe. He collapses into a seat. Relieved. Then he hears a familiar sound. Coo. The pigeon is already inside the bus. Dave completely loses it. He screams for help. Begs the driver to stop. The bus screeches to a halt. Dave jumps out and runs. The audience realizes there is absolutely no escaping this bird. ⸻ 🌳 ACT 5 — THE PARK Dave runs into a nearby park. Exhausted. Breathing heavily. He hides behind a giant statue. Silence. No flapping. No cooing. Nothing. For the first time all day, he believes he has finally escaped. The danger seems over. ⸻ 🍟 ACT 6 — THE REVEAL A single french fry suddenly falls from the sky. Landing directly at Dave’s feet. His face drops instantly. He already knows what it means. Slowly, he looks up. The pigeon stands triumphantly on top of the statue. Then another french fry falls. As it drops— The story flashes back in black and white. Months earlier. A tiny baby pigeon excitedly approaches its favorite french fry. Just before it reaches the snack— Dave casually grabs it and eats it. The baby pigeon watches in absolute heartbreak. The audience finally discovers the truth. This entire feud started because of one stolen french fry. ⸻ 💩 ACT 7 — REVENGE COMPLETE Back in the present. Dave stares at the pigeon in disbelief. After all this time… The pigeon is still angry. The pigeon slowly nods. Then delivers one final perfectly aimed attack. Direct hit. Dave stands frozen in defeat. The pigeon proudly celebrates from atop the statue. Cut to black.
```

### Topview Film Studio、実際に使ってみた... これはかなり使い勝手良いですね！✨


![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 İstem

```
アニメーションのアクションシーン、美しいドレスを着た美女と人型ロボットが、誰もいない舞踏会のホール内で激しく銃撃戦を繰り広げる大迫力のシーンとなる60秒のショートフィルムを生成してください。
魅力的なキャラクター、表現力のある映像、人型ロボットはホーミング銃弾を発射すると、その銃弾は噴煙航跡を引きながら美女を追尾する。
美女はホール内をパルクールのようなアクションで縦横無尽に三次元的な動きで回避行動を取りつつ、ときおり拳銃でロボットを射撃する。
カメラアングルとカメラワークは美女と人型ロボットを魅力的に演出するよう、派手な3D旋回系の動作で撮影を続ける。
---

2回目以降はプロンプト中の状況設定や被写体の動きなどをテキストで修正していっただけですが、ほぼ意図通りの映像を出してくるのが素晴らしい...

Text-to-Video なので参照画像無しにも関わらずここまでできるので、作品のラフな初期アイデア出し段階でもイメージを簡単に具体化できるところはメリット大きいです☺️

...PR案件ですが、個人的にはガチで課金して使ってるサービスなので、実際オススメ👍
Topview、もうすぐ Seedance 2.5 対応も入ってくるので、そっちも期待大！

Use image1 as the strict reference for Pyona’s face, facial proportions, light pastel-blue bob hairstyle, hair color, white cloud hair clip, blue fish hair clip, body proportions, age impression, exact outfit, styling, and overall recognizable identity. Do not redesign her. image2 = the clown enemy. Use image2 as the strict reference for the clown’s identity, proportions, terrifying costume, giant heavy axe, and overall menacing appearance. Preserve the same clown and same weapon consistently throughout. CORE ACTION RULE: Pyona must never block the axe blade with her bare body. She survives and fights through prediction, timing, footwork, body-angle control, narrow dodges, aerial repositioning, spacing, and fast counters. The clown must feel physically overwhelming, heavy, and destructive. Pyona stays alive and gains control through speed and precision, not brute force. ENVIRONMENT: A ruined urban battlefield at night, like a collapsed industrial ruin or destroyed concrete structure. Broken walls, exposed rebar, cracked ground, rubble piles, dust clouds, shattered concrete, and damaged surfaces everywhere. The axe attacks must visibly damage the environment: scraping the ground, throwing sparks and debris, cracking walls, carving trenches, and blasting rubble outward. Maintain clear spatial consistency. STYLE: Live-action photorealism with strong action readability. High-end cinematic fight energy, like a boss fight in a dark action film. Realistic body weight, believable recoil, sharp contact, strong debris simulation, and dangerous near-miss tension. No comedy. No magical powers. No fantasy effects. CAMERA: Use dynamic but readable cinematic camera coverage: - low-angle handheld push-ins - side tracking shots - medium rotating follow shots - brief wide shots for scale and aftermath - one brief slow-motion emphasis only during the most dangerous near-miss. Cuts may be dynamic, but action must remain clear, coherent, and easy to follow. TIMELINE: 0.0–3.8s — Opening 3-hit pressure sequence Start immediately in motion with no pause. The clown launches an aggressive three-part attack sequence: 1. a huge horizontal axe sweep toward Pyona, 2. an immediate reverse backhand sweep, 3. a heavy downward slam. The first swing scrapes the ground and throws sparks, broken stone, and debris. Pyona performs a fast lateral slip-step dodge, narrowly avoiding the blade as it passes just beside her shoulder. Without pause, the clown reverses into the second sweep. Pyona ducks and twists her upper body under the returning blade, avoiding it by centimeters. The clown then commits into the third attack, a brutal overhead slam. Pyona instantly side-jumps or rolls diagonally away just before impact. The axe slams into the ground and carves a trench, blasting dust and rubble upward. The whole opening must feel relentless, like Pyona is under constant lethal pressure with no breathing room. 3.8–6.8s — Fast first counter / control disruption As the axe is briefly stuck or slowed from the heavy slam, Pyona instantly takes the opening. She leaps lightly off broken rubble or a damaged concrete edge and darts in with a short, sharp three-hit counter: - first, a quick strike to the clown’s weapon-control arm or axe-handle area, - second, another fast disrupting hit to the grip / forearm zone, - third, a clean straight punch to the clown’s chest. These hits are not about overpowering the clown, but about disrupting his balance and force generation. The clown stumbles backward and crashes against part of a ruined wall, creating visible cracks and shaking loose concrete dust. Pyona lands and immediately side-steps out again, never staying directly in front of the axe. 6.8–10.2s — Most dangerous near-miss The clown recovers faster than expected and instantly retaliates with a violent reverse horizontal axe sweep aimed directly at Pyona’s torso. This is the most dangerous beat of Scene 1. Use one brief slow-motion emphasis here. Pyona sharply twists her waist and slides half a step sideways at the very last moment. The axe blade misses her body by only a few centimeters, slicing past the side of her outfit and strongly disturbing the hem / edge of her cropped top and jacket without actually injuring her. Ground fragments and dust fly through frame. The near miss must feel terrifyingly close. The slow motion should be very brief, then snap back to full speed immediately. 10.2–12.8s — Clean momentum-based knockout counter Using the dodge momentum from the near miss, Pyona spins fluidly into a fast, powerful roundhouse kick. The kick lands cleanly and forcefully on the clown’s side face / jaw. The impact must feel sharp and real. The clown is blasted backward and crashes through part of a ruined wall or major concrete section. Broken concrete, rubble chunks, and a heavy dust burst explode across the frame. Pyona lands balanced and alert, not posing, already ready for the next move. 12.8–15.0s — Cliffhanger ending Wide aftermath shot. Dust and debris settle around the collapsed wall area. For a brief moment it seems Pyona has finally created space. Then movement appears inside the wreckage. The clown plants one hand into the rubble, grabs the giant axe, and forcefully rises again from the debris, still dangerous and not defeated. Pyona remains in the foreground or mid-ground in a guarded stance, breathing hard, watching him carefully and maintaining distance. End on the clown partially risen in the rubble with the axe in hand, clearly setting up Scene 2. AUDIO: No dialogue. Use only strong cinematic fight sound design: heavy axe swings, metal scrape on stone, ground carving, sparks, concrete cracking, rubble spray, debris fall, air displacement, fast footwork, body impact, wall collapse, dusty low-end impact booms, and tense dark action ambience. No spoken lines. IMPORTANT RULES: Pyona must never tank the axe blade. She must always evade first, then counter. The clown’s axe must feel extremely heavy and lethal. The clown’s attacks must come in chained pressure, not one isolated swing at a time. Pyona’s counters must be short, precise, and opening-based. Environmental damage must match the axe impacts. Keep both characters visually consistent with image1 and image2. Scene 1 must end with the clown rising again, not fully defeated. NEGATIVE: No direct body-blocking of the axe, no weak impact, no fake contact, no floating, no teleporting, no wire-fu, no random extra enemies, no comedy, no magical powers, no repeated identical attack loops, no unreadable blurry action, no identity drift, no hairstyle change, no outfit redesign, no loss of the visible crop-top feel in Pyona’s outfit, no soft or low-energy choreography.

Time completely freezes.

As the world remains suspended, the camera moves fluidly around the frozen scene, capturing premium commercial-style macro close-ups of the apparel, fabric texture, stitching, logo, visor, wristbands, racket strings, shoes, and other athletic details. Use luxurious product lighting, shallow depth of field, crisp textures, smooth cinematic camera movement, and polished sports-commercial cinematography.

Time resumes naturally.

She completes the serve, lowers her racket, and walks forward with quiet confidence. The camera instantly whip-pans across the court, following the tennis ball's trajectory.

On the opposite side of the court, dozens of tennis balls rapidly fly into place, precisely arranging themselves to spell **"COMTEX"** in large uppercase letters.

End with a clean overhead hero shot showcasing the completed brand name made entirely of tennis balls. Use premium commercial pacing, seamless transitions, realistic ball physics, dynamic camera work, cinematic sound design, HDR lighting, natural motion blur, ultra-realistic textures, and luxury sports-ad production quality throughout.

### Summer Beach BBQ and Anime Character Sticker

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

> A complex multi-part video prompt for Seedance that blends live-action beach BBQ footage with a 2D anime sticker character, including detailed instructions for character consistency and interaction.

#### 📝 İstem

```
[Seedance 2.5 / 15 seconds / 9:16]

Use the reference image as the sole character reference. 15 seconds, 9:16 vertical, one cut, fixed camera. The camera is completely fixed from start to finish. Zooming, panning, tilting, rotating, handheld movement, cuts, transitions, reframing, slow motion, and freeze-frame effects are prohibited.

[Composition/Positioning]
Place a live-action BBQ grill and meat on the bone in the left 45% of the screen. Place a 2D character in the right 30% of the screen. Reserve about 20% of space in the center for tongs and moving the meat.

The character stands on the sandy beach on the right, always displaying their whole body from head to black flip-flops. Do not place the character in front of the grill, on top of the grill legs, or on the table. Leave clear space between the character and the grill, maintaining their standing position from start to finish.

Do not overlap the grill, meat, human hands, tongs, or gochujang bottle with the character's body. The character must not hide the meat or grill. Human hands or tongs must not unnaturally cross the character's face or body. Accurately maintain depth and foreground-background relationships between objects.

[Visual Style]
A comical video combining live-action BBQ footage at a beautiful midsummer seaside with a flat 2D animated sticker character.

Draw a blue sea, white waves, bright blue sky, golden sand, a distant coastline, and palm leaves swaying in the wind. Bright daytime natural light. Palm leaves, grill smoke, and the hem of the character's white shirt sway naturally in the sea breeze.

The background, sea, sand, grill, charcoal fire, smoke, flame, meat on the bone, fat, meat juice, gochujang, bottle, human hand, and metal tongs are all high-quality live-action expressions. Blown-out highlights, excessive bloom, haze, and exposure flickering are prohibited.

Only the character remains a flat 2D animated sticker until the end. Live-action, semi-live-action, 3D, CG figure, game model, Pixar style, or clay animation styles are prohibited. Maintain clear line drawings, cell shading, flat shadows, and thin white borders.

[Character Persistence]
Use the male character from the reference image exactly as they are. Fix the soft ash-silver wavy hair, bangs shape, hair length and volume, grey-blue eyes, young well-formed face, and slim long-limbed body type.

Fix the white sleeveless innerwear, sheer white short-sleeved open shirt, white drawcord, black paisley shorts, black flip-flops, silver necklace, earrings, bracelet, and ring.

Do not change the face, hairstyle, body type, clothing, patterns, colors, shoes, or accessories. Do not add hats, sunglasses, aprons, gloves, or new props. Do not duplicate the character. Do not add another 2D character.

The character is not coldly expressionless but moves very cutely and expressively. Clearly show large shining eyes, rosy cheeks, a happy smile, tilting the head, small shoulder-shaking movements, a sulking face with puffed cheeks, teary eyes, and frantic waving of hands. No standing stiffly.

[BBQ and Meat on the Bone]
Grill a large Tomahawk steak on the bone in the live-action BBQ grill on the left side of the screen. Long bones are clearly visible and physically connected to the meat. The meat is well-grilled, showing deep sear marks, savory char, glossy juices, melted fat, and light steam.

Do not make the meat raw, charred black, boneless, or deformed. Do not duplicate meat or bones. Do not let bones disappear midway.

A live-action adult woman's hand enters from the left edge of the screen. Maintain pink nail art, thin silver rings, and normal five fingers. Turn and lift the meat with metal BBQ tongs. Do not fuse the hand, fingers, ring, tongs, meat, or grill.

[Gochujang]
Use a transparent squeeze bottle with no label, text, or brand logo. The content is thick, glossy, deep reddish-brown gochujang. Do not make it bright red like ketchup or a watery liquid.

The gochujang flows in a thick continuous stream from the bottle nozzle and falls directly onto the meat surface. Do not make the sauce appear suddenly from mid-air. Do not leak from the bottom or sides of the bottle.

[00:00–00:03]
Start with a fixed wide composition. On the left, a Tomahawk steak on the bone is sizzling loudly. A woman's live-action hand slowly turns the meat once with metal tongs. Fat falls onto the charcoal, and a small flame rises for a moment. Thin smoke drifts in the sea breeze.

The 2D character on the right stares at the meat, their eyes sparkling large. They put their hands together under their chin, raise their shoulders, bend their knees slightly, and bounce twice. A small 2D drop of drool appears at the mouth for a moment and disappears.

[00:03–00:05]
The character finds the gochujang bottle. Their eyes sparkle, they tilt their head, and point at the bottle with one hand. They happily hold the bottle with both hands and hug it to their chest for a moment.

Then, with a mischievous smile and one eye closed, they point the nozzle toward the meat on the left. The character themselves does not move from their standing position on the right.

[00:05–00:07]
The character squeezes the bottle hard, pouring a large amount of gochujang onto the meat on the bone. A thick reddish-brown sauce extends continuously from the nozzle to the meat, creating a large zigzag pattern on the meat surface.

The sauce bubbles lightly with the heat and mixes with the meat juice to create a gloss. Maintain visibility of the bone and grill marks without completely hiding the whole meat.

The character smiles proudly, sticks out their tongue slightly, and shakes their shoulders cutely while squeezing the bottle. The woman's hand stops in surprise while holding the tongs.

[00:07–00:09]
The woman's hand quickly takes the gochujang bottle with the tongs and returns it to the left. Immediately after, she lightly taps the top of the character's head once with the tip of the closed tongs.

The tip of the tongs clearly and lightly touches the character's hair. At the moment of contact, a small yellow 2D star-shaped impact effect appears over the head. Do not hit hard. Do not make it painful. Make it a completely cute cartoon-style reprimand.

The moment they are hit, the character's head tilts to the right, their silver hair bounces, and their shoulders and torso shake greatly to the left and right. Knees bend, and they spread their arms to balance. Feet do not leave the sand, and they do not move toward the grill.

They puff their cheeks, close their eyes tight, and shed a single small tear. Clearly show the cute shaking movement of the whole body, not just the head, for about 1 second.

[00:09–00:11]
The woman's hand returns the tongs to the grill and lifts a bite-sized piece of grilled meat with gochujang on it. The lifted meat shows grill marks, juice, fibers, and thick gochujang.

The tongs pass through the center space and carry the meat to the front of the character's mouth. The character sulks about being hit on the head, crosses their arms, turns their face away, and puffs their cheeks.

Immediately they glance at the meat with just their eyes. Their eyes sparkle again, they uncross their arms, put both hands behind their back, and lean their upper body slightly forward. They open their mouth wide and approach the meat.

[00:11–00:12]
The character clearly [eats] the meat
```

Red and White Swordswoman Red Energy Sword Combat Combo
Scene Header: Day · Interior · White Geometric Space 
 · monochrome minimalist · high-key diffuse light · red energy accent

Shot 1 | 0-2s · Medium Shot · Stance
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman stands in the center of a white geometric space. Long white hair, Cheongsam-style outfit, red flower ornament. A red energy blade materializes in the right hand, in a floating ready state. The left hand is lowered naturally in a relaxed posture.
Dialogue: None

Shot 2 | 2-4s · Medium Shot · Spin Slash
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman twists her body clockwise on the axis of her waist. The red energy blade in the right hand draws a large arc from the right side forward for a slash. A red translucent motion blur trail leaves a trajectory of the sword. Her white long hair and Cheongsam flow due to the centrifugal force of the rotation.
Dialogue: None

Shot 3 | 4-6s · Low Angle Medium Shot · Low Sweep
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman sinks into a low posture with her knees on the ground. The red energy blade rotates horizontally around her body just above the ground, drawing a wide semicircular arc. Red light reflects on the floor of the white geometric space.
Dialogue: None

Shot 4 | 6-8s · Medium Shot · Upward Slash to Mid-air Jump
Visual: As the Red and White Swordswoman rises from the low posture, the red energy blade draws a vertical arc from bottom to top. She jumps into the air, and her body rises. The energy blade follows and floats above her head.
Dialogue: None

Shot 5 | 8-10s · Medium Shot · Mid-air Inverted Slash
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman flips her body in the air. The red energy blade draws a trajectory smashing downward from above her head. Her body rotates elegantly in the air, and her long white hair flows. The pillars of the white geometric space flow in the background.
Dialogue: None

Shot 6 | 10-12s · Low Angle Medium Shot · Landing Impact
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman lands in a low posture with one hand on the ground. The red energy blade strikes the ground, creating a shockwave with orange sparks and red light particles scattering. The afterglow of the landing impact remains in the white geometric space.
Dialogue: None

Shot 7 | 12-14s · Medium Shot · Return to Stance
Visual: The Red and White Swordswoman stands up, the red energy blade returns to her right hand, and the energy converges. She returns to an upright stance. The silence of the white geometric space returns.
Dialogue: None

SFX: Energy resonance sound + sword wind sound + landing impact sound + sparking fire sounds

Visual style direction: monochrome minimalist environment with red energy accent, high-key diffuse lighting, cel-shaded 3D anime aesthetic, translucent red arc motion trails for energy blade trajectory, orange spark particles and red light particles on impact, elegant and precise combat choreography.

[Generation Mode]

Seedance 2.0 Fast

[Video Specifications]

Strictly generate a 15-second video, 16:9 aspect ratio, with three clear shots.

[Overall Style]

Poetic Xianxia visual illusion comedy, realistic cinematic high-definition texture. Uses classical Chinese negative space composition, restrained deadpan performance, soft morning light, low-saturation jade green and ivory white tones, and fine film grain. Borrows from Buster Keaton-style spatial illusions, revealing the truth through controlled camera rotation rather than relying on failed magic for laughs. The entire film is set in a pure ancient Chinese world with no modern elements.

[Character Locking]

Use @Image 1 and @Image 2 to strictly lock character identity, face, hairstyle, body proportions, clothing, shoes, and accessories.

Character ID A | Senior Sister Sword Immortal

An East Asian female aged 25-30, oval face, natural fair skin tone, dark almond eyes, black long hair half-tied and fixed with a white jade hairpin, tall and slender build. Wearing white embroidered silk Hanfu with semi-transparent layered wide sleeves, silver waist seal, jade pendant, and white cloth boots.

Character ID B | Junior Sister

An East Asian female aged 20-25, rounded and agile face, black hair in braids, petite build. Wearing bluish-green linen Hanfu, dark belt, wooden hairpin, and black cloth shoes.

[Shot 1 | 0-5s | Wide Shot or Long Shot]

The frame is intentionally rotated 90 degrees clockwise, making a steep mountain wall appear horizontally as the ground. 

Character ID A: Senior Sister, appearing in full from head to toe, seems to be walking upright and calmly along the vertical cliff with hands behind her back, while her hair and sleeves naturally hang toward the vast abyss in the frame.

Character ID B: Junior Sister seems to be hanging onto an ancient pine tree below.

[Shot 2 | 5-10s | Medium Shot or Cowboy Shot]

The same Junior Sister in green linen Hanfu looks up and asks: "Senior Sister, is this 'Treading the Wall to Heaven'?"

The same Senior Sister in white silk Hanfu continues walking calmly and replies indifferently: "If the heart is upright, heaven and earth will naturally be level."

The background remains the same mountain wall, pine tree, floating mist, stone platform, and distant roofed water pavilion; the incorrect vertical orientation must be maintained without altering character anatomy.

[Shot 3 | 10-15s | Close-up or Extreme Close-up]

The camera smoothly rotates 90 degrees counter-clockwise to restore the true horizontal line, revealing that the Senior Sister is simply walking on a flat stone platform next to a low wall, while the Junior Sister is just casually leaning against the pine tree.

Junior Sister says expressionlessly: "Senior Sister, the world is level, but the camera is crooked."

Extreme Close-up: The Senior Sister's hard-maintained dignified smile instantly freezes, one eyebrow twitches slightly, and she then replies as if nothing happened: "This is to test your eyesight."

Ends with a dry pluck of a guqin string and the sound of a pine cone falling onto the stone slab.

[Technical Requirements]

Strict total duration of 15 seconds, 16:9 aspect ratio, three clear shots, original Mandarin dialogue, stable character faces and clothing, continuous scene geometry. Physics of mist, silk fabric, hair, pine trees, and gravity must be realistic and consistent. Do not generate on-screen subtitles. Only two visible characters should appear throughout.

[Negative Prompts]

blurry, bad quality, low quality, low resolution, noisy, jpeg artifacts, watermark, text, error; deformed, mutated, bad anatomy, poorly drawn hands, bad composition, out of frame, disfigured; inconsistent character, changing clothes, face morphing, background shift, glitching cuts, disappearing props

### Astronaut to Apartment Match Cut Transition

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> A sophisticated 15-second video prompt featuring a continuous motion match cut from an astronaut in space to a person in a VR headset.

#### 📝 İstem

```
FORMAT 15s / free rhythm / 1 MATCH CUT / CONTINUOUS MOVE UNTIL MATCH CUT + IMMEDIATE ACTION FROM FIRST FRAME

SUBJECTS: A lone astronaut in a damaged EVA suit desperately repairs a failing spacecraft while floating above Earth. The same person is later revealed sitting in a small apartment wearing casual clothes, where a VR headset appears only after the match cut and is removed in one uninterrupted motion.

ENVIRONMENT: Outer space above Earth’s glowing blue atmosphere, drifting debris, blinking warning lights, frozen silence broken by suit alarms. Transition into a dim but cozy apartment filled with scattered electronics, coffee mugs, and soft morning light spilling through curtains.

MOOD: High-stakes survival dissolves into everyday reality without breaking movement or spatial continuity.

COLOR LOGIC: Naturalistic Film Print Emulation.

⸻

TIMELINE

0:00–0:07

One continuous handheld-style zero-gravity camera move, WS gradually collapsing into MCU. The astronaut tumbles toward a damaged satellite panel as warning lights flash across the visor. One gloved hand desperately reaches for a sparking cable while the other stabilizes against the hull. Loose debris slowly spins past the lens. The Earth fills half the background, slowly rotating beneath. The camera floats with the subject, maintaining believable inertia and subtle drift while slowly shifting from a 24mm to 35mm perspective.

SFX: (oxygen warning beeps, suit breathing, metallic creaks, distant electrical crackle, gloved impacts against metal, radio static)

Cold sunlight creates sharp highlights across the visor while Earth’s blue glow softly fills the shadows.

⸻

0:07–0:11

Without cutting, the camera tightens into a dead-on CU. Sparks burst inches from the astronaut’s helmet as a damaged panel begins separating from the spacecraft. Just before impact, a woman’s voice calmly calls,

“Alex…”

Then louder,

“ALEX!”

The astronaut exhales in annoyance instead of panic.

Everything suddenly enters slow motion.

Floating debris nearly freezes.

The separating panel hangs motionless.

Only the astronaut continues moving at normal speed.

The camera slowly orbits clockwise around the helmet while maintaining identical framing and distance.

Instead of reaching for the failing equipment, both hands rise toward the helmet in one smooth, deliberate motion.

No headset or gaming device is visible anywhere in space.

Maintain identical face size, hand position, lens distance, head angle, and clockwise motion until the match cut.

SFX: (radio distortion stretching into silence, slowed warning alarms, breathing, woman’s voice echoing “Alex… ALEX…”, ambient hum fading)

⸻

0:11–0:15

MATCH CUT.

CU to MS.

The rising hands occupy the exact same screen position as the helmet instantly becomes a VR headset in a modest apartment.

The motion never stops.

For the first time, the VR headset is visible.

The subject grips both sides and smoothly lifts
```

[Main Character] = Black-haired girl
[Opponent/Target] = Brown-haired girl

15 seconds. Japanese full-color anime. High-density 2D animation with a 500 million yen budget scale. Cinematic backlighting, hard shadows, cold ambient light, rapid zoom-out, facial close-ups. Ultra-wide pull back. Countless [Anomaly Gimmicks] on walls, floor, ceiling, and desks all glow red at once. The [Main Character] becomes a black silhouette against the backlight.

[Shot 7: Declaration]
Rapid zoom back to the [Main Character]'s face. With an overwhelmingly calm smile, she looks at the camera and says, 'I saw everything.' Stop the last frame with red backlighting.

Preserve the exact same facial identity, facial proportions, skin tone, hairstyle, hair color, age impression, body proportions, clothing, accessories, and overall presence from the uploaded character image. The protagonist must remain visually consistent throughout the entire film. Use the uploaded five action reference videos only to control the corresponding card actions. These action reference videos must influence only the hand movements, finger articulation, grip, deck handling, card trajectories, motion rhythm, timing, release force, catching motion, and finishing poses. Do not inherit any character appearance, wardrobe, background, framing, lighting, composition, camera angle, or visual style from the action reference videos. SCENE: A real upscale casino interior with a deep green felt card table positioned at the center. The distant casino environment remains dim and subdued, with only faint silhouettes of tables, chairs, chandeliers, and architectural elements visible in the darkness. The setting feels premium, restrained, and cinematic. LIGHTING: A single focused warm-white overhead spotlight illuminates the protagonist’s upper body, hands, cards, and the green felt table. The rest of the casino falls into near darkness. Add a very subtle soft frontal fill light only to preserve detail in the eyes, finger joints, and card edges without flattening the dramatic shadows. The lighting must create realistic depth, directional contrast, natural falloff, and physically believable shadow behavior. The cards, hands, and fingers must cast consistent and dynamically changing shadows onto the green felt table. As cards move, flip, arc, rotate, and fall, their brightness, reflections, motion blur, occlusion, and shadows must respond naturally to their angle and position under the overhead light. Avoid flat lighting, decorative rim lighting, fantasy glow, neon effects, or unmotivated light sources. ACTION CHAIN: CARD WATERFALL PULL Strictly reference the uploaded "[Card Waterfall]" video for the movement only. Begin already in motion. Extreme close-up from a front-right angle near hand level. Show only both hands, part of the sleeves, the deck, and a small area of green felt. One hand releases the cards in a continuous controlled stream into the other hand. Preserve the exact rhythm, hand spacing, release pattern, falling arc, and receiving motion from the reference. The receiving hand catches the full deck and squares it cleanly. The overhead light creates thin moving highlights along the card edges and layered moving shadows across the receiving hand and table. ONE-HANDED CHARLIER CUT Strictly reference the clean one-handed Charlier cut segment from the uploaded "[Charlier Cut]" video for the movement only. Use only one complete Charlier cut. Do not add any extra one-handed flourishes or multi-packet variations. Close hand shot from the protagonist’s left-front side, slightly below hand level. The frame includes the operating hand, the other hand resting naturally near the table edge, a small portion of the torso, and the green felt table. The deck separates into two packets, the lower packet drops, the finger pushes through, the packets pass, and the deck closes as one. The camera remains locked off. One side of the hand is lit by the overhead light while the opposite side falls into natural shadow, clearly showing the thickness and layers of the cards. VERTICAL TWO-HAND FLOURISH Strictly reference the uploaded "[Cardistry/Flourish]" video for the movement only, specifically the segment where the cards form a continuous stepped vertical chain between the upper hand and the lower receiving hand. Do not include the crossed-arm ending. Front-facing medium close-up at chest height. Frame the upper hand, the vertical chain of cards, the lower receiving hand, and part of the torso. The face should be only partially visible or remain secondary to the card action. Preserve the exact hand spacing, stepped structure, curved path, release rhythm, downward flow, and final catching motion from the reference. As the cards pass through the overhead beam, each card briefly moves from shadow into highlight and back into shadow, producing flickering natural edge highlights and layered moving shadows on the hands, torso, and table. TABLE RIBBON SPREAD AND PROGRESSIVE TURNOVER Strictly reference the uploaded "Ribbon Spread" video for the movement only. Use an overhead 90-degree top-down view. The frame contains only both hands and the full ribbon spread on the green felt table. The protagonist spreads the deck smoothly into one long evenly spaced ribbon with card backs facing upward, then progressively turns the entire ribbon over from one end to the other with a continuous finger motion, revealing the card faces in sequence. Preserve the reference movement direction, spreading speed, card spacing, ribbon length, turnover rhythm, and finish. The camera remains completely static. The overhead spotlight creates a soft oval pool of light across the center of the table, with fine contact shadows between the cards and a traveling hand shadow moving along the ribbon during the turnover. Multiple one-handed cuts in rapid succession, the silver ring flashing with every movement. Wide shot of him seated at the card table with the dark casino behind him, alternating with tight close-ups of his hands and a sharp side profile. He suddenly launches the entire deck high into the air. The cards explode upward, spinning rapidly and catching the overhead light. HERO MOMENT. The instant the cards reach their highest point, time slows dramatically into a bullet-time effect. The camera orbits around him as hundreds of cards hang suspended in mid-air, rotating slowly under the light. He remains completely still, then turns his eyes directly toward the camera for the first time—cold, controlled, unflinching. Time suddenly accelerates again and the cards begin falling rapidly around him. Without looking away from the camera, he reaches into the collapsing cloud of cards and cleanly catches a single card between two fingers. All remaining cards fall onto the table. Close-up: he slowly turns the captured card toward the camera—the seven of clubs. Faintest smile. Cut to black. Jazz fades into AND EDITING RULES: Each action must be fully completed before the next cut. Use clean hard cuts only. No dissolves, no morphing transitions, no flash transitions, no AI-style warping, and no continuous blending between different actions. Every new shot begins with the hands and deck already in the correct starting pose for that action. Do not visibly transform one grip into another on screen. AUDIO: It should remain subtle and never overpower the card sounds. Every action must have synchronized, realistic card audio: friction, sliding, snapping, dropping, spreading, flipping, flying, and not generate background music for the entire video except for SFX. VISUAL STYLE: Ultra-realistic live-action cinema. Real actor, real casino environment, real card material, real hand structure, and physically believable movement. Skin must show natural pores, slight tonal unevenness, knuckle creases, and realistic tension in the fingers. Cards must have believable thickness, slight edge wear, realistic flexibility, and consistent physical inertia. Use restrained exposure, soft highlight roll-off, deep layered shadows, realistic focus transitions, subtle sensor noise, and natural motion blur.

### Realistic Outfit Change Video Sequence

![中文](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-中文-red)

> A highly detailed 12-second vertical video prompt for Seedance 2.0 designed to create a realistic single-frame outfit change using physics-based movements.

#### 📝 İstem

```
[General Requirements] 12-second realistic single outfit change short video, 9:16 vertical screen. Strictly replicate the movement logic of the reference video 'Balance Outfit Change': the female lead balances on one leg, uses the outer side of her lifted foot to hold a bundle of 'shoes + clothes', tosses the bundle into the air, quickly retracts her leg to build power, and performs a single high-speed kick that accurately hits the target; the outfit change occurs in the exact frame where the foot and the clothes overlap. The change must be a hard cut in one frame, not a gradient, transformation, or clothes gradually covering the body. No dialogue, no narration, no subtitles, no watermarks, keep the background music.
[Character Lock] The lead is an adult woman over 25, using @1. Facial features, skin tone, hairstyle, height, and body proportions remain consistent throughout, no face-swapping, no age change, no body shape change. Movements feature realistic center of gravity, muscle exertion, breathing patterns, and slight balance swaying.
[Outfit Lock] Before change: Black short-sleeved T-shirt, lavender flare pants, black and white sneakers. After change: The lead instantly switches to the complete look of preset #1, strictly maintaining the colors, cut, material, and styling of #1. Only one outfit change occurs in the entire video, and the second look lasts until the end without adding, removing, or replacing any clothing, shoes, or accessories.
[Starting Clothing Bundle] The opening prop is not a single shoe, but a compact, weighted bundle consisting of the shoes and folded clothes corresponding to preset #1. The shoe outlines are on top, with folded fabric supporting the shoes from below and some fabric edges hanging naturally. The bundle is supported by the outer side of the lead's left sneaker, the outer instep, and near the ankle. It is not placed on the toe, not worn, and not floating. Once tossed, the shoes and clothes must fly together as a single unit without scattering.
[Scene] Minimalist modern residential interior with white walls and light gray hard flooring. Background includes a white wardrobe, door, washing machine, and a few household items. Natural window light mixed with soft indoor light; furniture, background, and lighting direction remain constant. Realistic mobile phone video texture, skin retains natural texture, clothing and shoe materials are clear.
[Close-up Fixed Camera] Fixed tight full-body shot, camera distance approximately 2.1–2.3 meters from the lead, height about 1.05 meters, 30–32mm equivalent focal length, nearly eye-level with a slight low angle. The lead occupies about 80% of the screen height, positioned slightly to the right to leave space for the left leg lift and clothing bundle trajectory. About 8% space at the top and 4% at the bottom. The person, support leg, lifted left leg, and airborne bundle stay fully in frame. Stationary throughout, no zooming, panning, or tracking cuts.
[Strict Time Storyboard] [00:00-00:00.90] The lead stands sideways, right leg as the sole support, right knee slightly bent; left leg lifted sideways to near horizontal. Left foot turned slightly inward so the outer side of the sneaker faces up like a tilted tray. The bundle of shoes and clothes is held steadily by the outer foot. She looks at the bundle with focus. Arms naturally raised for balance. No eye contact with camera.
[00:00.90-00:01.05] A short, clear upward flick using only the left ankle and outer foot, tossing the bundle diagonally upward. A light toss, not a full kick. The bundle leaves the foot, rising along a short parabolic path. Fabric edges expand slightly due to air, but the unit stays together. Accompanying 'whoosh' sound.
[00:01.05-00:01.45] Bundle rises to peak. Lead immediately retracts left leg, knee bending toward chest/abdomen in a compact ready stance. Body tilts slightly right, arms spread with gravity shift. Eyes track the clothes, no looking at camera.
[00:01.45-00:01.65] Bundle begins to descend. Lead finishes retracting leg, pausing for no more than 0.2s to judge the landing point.
[00:01.65-00:01.85] Lead performs one formal high kick, succeeding on the first try. Left leg explodes diagonally upward, knee opening rapidly, shin and toe fully extending in 0.2s. Fast, clean, sharp kick with natural motion blur. Left foot accurately meets the falling bundle, no misses or corrections.
[00:01.85, Instant Hard Cut] Frame of contact between foot and bundle center triggers a seamless hard cut. Pre-contact frame: Lead in black T-shirt and lavender pants; bundle in air. Post-contact frame: Lead in preset #1; bundle completely gone. No transition frames, no blending, no gradual growth. Just a hard cut.
[00:01.85-00:02.80] Hold high kick peak for 0.1s, then lower leg. She looks at her foot first, then slowly looks at the camera with confident smile.
[00:02.80-00:04.10] First siren-style fashion pose: S-curve body, one hand on waist, the other passing through hair. Looks away then back at camera with a mysterious smile.
[00:04.10-00:05.40] Two-thirds profile stance, feet staggered, shoulders back. She looks at camera, tilts head, and blinks slowly with a playful, high-fashion vibe.
[00:05.40-00:06.60] Slow turn showing back to camera. Hair slides along shoulders. Turns head back over shoulder with a slight smirk.
[00:06.60-00:09.30] Walks away from camera toward depth of room with 3-4 steps of a relaxed runway gait. Fixed camera captures full back view.
[00:09.30-00:10.40] Stops in background, one foot pointed sideways, still back to camera, then looks over shoulder with a mysterious side-smile and quick wink.
[00:10.40-00:12.00] Climax: On the final beat, a clean half-turn with a hair flip, stepping toward camera into a two-thirds frontal pose. One hand on waist, other near chin, chin up, staring at camera with direct siren gaze. Final 0.3s freeze frame.
[Audio] 00:00-01:85 ethereal female humming with reverb, ambient friction/whoosh sounds. At 01:85, heavy electronic drum drop with a deep 'thump'. 01:85-12:00 continuous electronic beat with walking/clothing friction sounds. Ending with an upward sweep. No dialogue.
[Strict Negative Constraints] No gradient changes, no dissolving, no gradual clothes growth, no fabric crawling on body, no color spreading, no old clothes fading; no intermediate states. Hard cut at contact: 100% old outfit before, 100% preset #1 after. No unauthorized modification to #1.
```

The cave entrance opens from a high mountain cliff into a vast dramatic landscape of jagged mountains, deep valleys, distant waterfalls, rolling clouds and pale stormy daylight. The camera slowly tracks backward toward the entrance as the enormous dragon walks forward out of the black cave interior. The dragon’s wings remain completely folded against both sides of its body. SHOT 3 — REAR TAKEOFF AND FLIGHT, 9–15 SECONDS: Clean hard cut to an epic wide rear view positioned behind and slightly above the dragon and rider. The dragon accelerates into a powerful run toward the cave opening while its wings remain folded enough to pass safely through the cave. The dragon reaches the cliff edge and makes one forceful leap completely out of the cave. Only after clearing the rock opening, it spreads both enormous wings to their full symmetrical width. The sudden wing expansion pushes mist, dust and loose stones outward from the cave entrance. The rider leans forward, grips the reins and remains firmly attached to the saddle. The dragon performs one enormous downward wingbeat, catches the air and transitions naturally into stable forward flight. Loose stones fall from the cliff beneath it. The camera rapidly follows from behind as the dragon and rider soar away over the deep valley between jagged mountain peaks. The long tail streams naturally behind the dragon and the rider’s cloak whips in the wind. ### Mini DV Retro Bitki Bakımı ASMR Vlog

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> Elde çekim titremesi ve odak kaymalarıyla özgünlük kazandırılmış, ASMR sesleri eşliğinde 90'ların nostaljik video kamera tarzında bitki bakımı videosu oluşturur.

#### 📝 İstem

```
KAMERA / GÖRÜNTÜ: Kişinin kendi tarafından çekilmiş elde mini DV kamera görüntüleri. Hafif el titremesi, ara sıra odak kaymaları, kusurlu kadraj, doğal yakınlaştırma ayarları, yumuşak kaset benzeri görüntü kalitesi, hafif gren, parlak pencere önü gün ışığından kaynaklanan gerçekçi otomatik pozlama değişimleri. Doğal cilt tonları, hafif hareket bulanıklığı; cilalı sinematik görüntülerden ziyade otantik tüketici tipi video kamera estetiği.
STİL: Hafif ASMR öğeleri içeren samimi ve hızlı bir bitki bakımı vlog'u. Rahat bir tempo, minimal diyalog, doğal anlar. Tatmin edici günlük seslere odaklanma: toprak küreme, küçük saksı sürtünmesi, sukulent yapraklarının birbirine değmesi, nazikçe bastırma.
ÖZNE: 20'li yaşlarının ortasında genç bir kadın, düz bir hırka, gevşek bir topuz yapılmış saçlar, minimal takılar, görünürde logo veya markalı ürün yok. Hızlı bir iş için sakin, neşeli bir enerji.
MEKAN: Parlak bir sabah vakti küçük bir mutfak penceresi önü. Doğal gün ışığı, sıradan bir pişmiş toprak saksı, markasız bir toprak torbası, kadrajda görünür marka ismi veya logo yok.
SENARYO:
→ (3 sn, sabit orta çekim) Kamerayı pencere kenarına yerleştirir, küçük bir saksıya toprak doldurur. "Bu küçük arkadaş için hızlı bir saksı değişimi."
→ (3 sn, yakın çekim) Sukulenti nazikçe eski saksısından çıkarır.
→ (3 sn, elde çekim) Taze toprağa yerleştirir, kök kısmını nazikçe bastırır. "İşte, şimdi çok daha mutlu."
→ (3 sn, detay çekimi) Yaprakların üzerindeki fazla toprağı fırçalar. Diyalog yok.
→ (3 sn, sıcak kapanış çekimi) Saksıyı tekrar pencere kenarına koyar, kameraya gülümser. "Bir sonraki bitkide görüşürüz." Kayıt biterken el lensi kapatır.
SES NOTLARI: Doğal ortam sesleri, toprak küreme ve yaprak sürtünme sesleri net bir şekilde duyulmalı. Diyaloglar, küçük bir kişisel kitleye konuşuyormuş gibi sessiz ve gündelik olmalı.
GERÇEKÇİLİK NOTLARI: Otantik vücut dili, doğal göz kırpma, samimi ve neşeli bir gülümseme, kusurlu kadraj, odak nefes alıp vermeleri, parlak gün ışığı değişimleri. Kadrajın hiçbir yerinde telif hakkı olan karakterler, logolar, marka isimleri veya tanınabilir halk figürleri bulunmamalı. Tamamen özgün kişisel vlog içeriği, yapay zeka üretimi veya ticari tarzda değil.
```

### Sinematik Moda Dergisi Kapak Çekimi

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> Seedance 2.0 için tasarlanmış; stüdyo ışık geçişleri, senkronize pozlar ve final editoryal kapak çekimi içeren, moda fotoğraf çekimini kurgulayan profesyonel bir video istemi.

#### 📝 İstem

```
0:00–0:03 | Stüdyo Girişi

Parlak stüdyo ışıkları birer birer yanıyor. O, kendinden emin bir şekilde kusursuz beyaz sete adım atarken flaşlar patlamaya başlıyor. Asistanlar hızla blazer ceketini düzeltirken fotoğrafçı kamerasını kaldırıyor. Hızlı kırbaç pan geçişleri beklentiyi artırıyor.

0:03–0:07 | Editoryal Poz Sekansı
Her kamera flaşıyla birlikte seri moda pozları:
Eller blazer ceplerinde
Omzunun üzerinden bakış
Yönetmen koltuğunda zarif bir oturuş
Çene hafifçe yukarıda, doğrudan göz teması
Podyum modeli gibi kameraya doğru yürüyüş
Her poz bir saniyeden kısa sürüyor; yüksek kamera deklanşör sesleri ve beyaz flaş geçişleriyle senkronize ediliyor.

0:07–0:11 | Kapak Anı
Arka plan; kusursuz eşleşmeli kesmeler kullanılarak minimalist beyaz, cesur siyah, metalik gümüş ve derin kızıl renkler arasında dönüşüyor. Göz temasını hiç bozmadan kendinden emin bir şekilde poz değiştirirken rüzgar makineleri saçlarını havalandırıyor. Kamera, ultra geniş moda çekimleri ile yakın güzellik çekimleri arasında geçiş yapıyor.

0:11–0:15 | Final Kapak Çekimi
Müzik kısa süreliğine yavaşlıyor. Son ve güçlü bir poz veriyor, tam bir özgüvenle doğrudan lense bakıyor. Kamera, art arda patlayan flaşlarla yavaşça aşırı yakın güzellik çekimine giriyor. Çerçeve, tıpkı lüks bir dergi kapağı gibi donuyor; etrafında zarif editoryal tipografi beliriyor ve son vuruşla ekran kararıyor.
```

### Lüks Parfüm Butiği Reklamı

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

> Sürekli bir takip çekimi ve sıcak ortam aydınlatması içeren, üst düzey bir parfüm mağazası reklamı için detaylı sinematik istem.

#### 📝 İstem

```
Stil: Ultra fotogerçekçi, lüks reklam, sinematik, tek bir sürekli takip çekimi, premium parfüm butiği, sıcak ortam aydınlatması, gerçekçi insan hareketi, 35mm lens, sığ alan derinliği, HDR, 8K, pürüzsüz gimbal stabilizasyonu.

Sahne 1 (0–3 sn) – Mağazaya Giriş

Üzerinde vücuda oturan siyah bir tişört ve koyu renkli kot pantolon olan şık bir genç adam, lüks bir parfüm butiğine giriyor. Kamera, o özgüvenle içeri girerken sol ön takip açısından onu izliyor. Premium cam parfüm şişeleriyle dolu zarif ahşap raflar, sıcak LED aydınlatma altında parlıyor. Cilalı beyaz mermer zemin, ışıkları yansıtarak premium bir atmosfer yaratıyor.

Sahne 2 (3–6 sn) – Koridorda Yürüyüş

Kamera, o parfüm reyonunda yavaşça yürürken ve koleksiyona gelişigüzel bakarken yanında pürüzsüz bir şekilde ilerliyor. Yüzlerce lüks parfüm şişesi, aydınlatılmış raflara düzenli bir şekilde yerleştirilmiş. Cam şişeler üzerinde yumuşak yansımalar, temiz modern iç mekan, sinematik alan derinliği, gerçekçi vücut hareketi, doğal yürüme temposu.

Sahne 3 (6–10 sn) – Koleksiyona Göz Atma

Kamera hafifçe yaklaşırken o, başını raflara doğru çevirerek premium parfümleri inceliyor. Yakın çekimler; altın rengi sıvılı zarif parfüm şişelerini, kristal netliğinde camı, metalik kapakları, arka planda yumuşak bokeh etkisini, sıcak lüks perakende aydınlatmasını ve gerçekçi yüz ifadelerini yakalıyor.

Sahne 4 (10–13 sn) – Final Sahnesi

O, özgüvenle vitrinin önünden geçmeye devam ederken kamera yavaşça yaklaşıyor. Lüks butik arka plana doğru uzanarak aydınlatılmış parfüm raflarını ve premium bir showroom'u gözler önüne seriyor. Pürüzsüz sinematik hareket, zarif atmosfer, lüks moda reklamı estetiği, ultra gerçekçi detaylar, temiz kompozisyon, üst düzey renk düzenleme, metin yok, filigran yok, logo yok.
```
